C.2
Proof of
Theorem

2.2

The proof relies on the following lemma, which establishes an upper bound on the accumulated primal gap provided that the discrepancy sequence
\{H_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
exhibits a non-asymptotic decay rate.

Lemma C.7
.

Suppose
Assumptions

1.1
and
1.4
hold. Let
\{x_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a primal LMO sequence with stepsize
\tau_{t}=(t+1)^{-a}
generated by the update rule (
15a
), let
\{y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}\subset{\cal Y}
be any dual sequence, and let
\beta_{t}=C(t+1)^{-b}
.
Suppose
1\geq a>b>0
,
C\geq 0
,
\max\{C,\mu\}>0
and we have

H_{t}\leq\frac{M_{t}}{(t+1)^{c}},\quad\forall t\geq 0

for some non-decreasing, positive sequence
\{M_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
and a constant
c
satisfying
b<c\leq 2a-b
if
C>0
and
0<c\leq 2a
if
C=0
and
\mu>0
.
Then, for any
T\geq 1
,

\displaystyle G_{\cal X}^{\Opt}(x_{T})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(1-b)T^{a+b-1}}+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2CM_{t}}+CL_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{(2-c+b)(C+\mu)T^{(2a-b+c-2)/2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2\mu M_{t}}+\mu L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{(2-c)(C+\mu)T^{(2a+c-2)/2}}.

Proof.

Since
0<a\leq 1
, it holds that

1-\tau_{t}=\frac{(t+1)^{a}-1}{(t+1)^{a}}\leq\frac{t^{a}}{(t+1)^{a}}.

We also note that by
Lemma

A.1

(vi)
, for any
t\geq 0
,

f_{t}(x_{t})-f_{t}(x^{\star})+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t}}{2}\geq\left(f(x_{t})-\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t}}{2}\right)-f(x^{\star})+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t}}{2}\geq f(x_{t})-f(x^{\star})\geq 0.

(
31
)

Similar to
Lemma

C.4
, one can prove that for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle 2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{\frac{2\tau_{t}^{2}H_{t}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}}+\frac{L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{2}+\frac{L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{2(\beta_{t}+\mu)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2CM_{t}}+CL_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{2(C+\mu)(t+1)^{(2a-b+c)/2}}+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2\mu M_{t}}+\mu L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{2(C+\mu)(t+1)^{(2a+c)/2}}.

Hence,
Lemma

A.7
implies

\displaystyle f_{t+1}(x_{t+1})-f_{t+1}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+2)^{b}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{t^{a}}{(t+1)^{a}}\left(f_{t}(x_{t})-f_{t}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD^{2}}{2(t+1)^{b}}\right)+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{a+b}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2CM_{t}}+CL_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{2(C+\mu)(t+1)^{(2a-b+c)/2}}+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2\mu M_{t}}+\mu L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{2(C+\mu)(t+1)^{(2a+c)/2}}.

We deduce that

\displaystyle t^{a}\left(f_{t}(x_{t})-f_{t}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{b}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(t-1)^{a}\left(f_{t-1}(x_{t-1})-f_{t-1}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD^{2}}{2t^{b}}\right)+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2t^{b}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2CM_{t-1}}+CL_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{2(C+\mu)t^{(c-b)/2}}+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2\mu M_{t-1}}+\mu L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{2(C+\mu)t^{c/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{t}\left(\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2i^{b}}+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2CM_{i-1}}+CL_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{2(C+\mu)i^{(c-b)/2}}+\frac{4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2\mu M_{i-1}}+\mu L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}}{2(C+\mu)i^{c/2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}t^{1-b}}{2(1-b)}+\frac{\left(4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2CM_{t}}+CL_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}\right)t^{(2-c+b)/2}}{(2-c+b)(C+\mu)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\left(4L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2\mu M_{t}}+\mu L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{C+\mu}\right)t^{(2-c)/2}}{(2-c)(C+\mu)},

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
(c-b)/2\leq a-b<1
. By (
31
), we have that

\displaystyle f(x_{t})-f(x^{\star})

\displaystyle\leq f_{t}(x_{t})-f_{t}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{b}},

which concludes the proof.
∎

.

Appendix D
Proofs of
Section

3

We start the analysis with establishing an upper bound on
H_{t}
, which through
Lemma

A.6
will then be used in
Lemma

D.2
to provide a bound on the dual gap.

Lemma D.1
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

2
under
\tau_{t}\in(0,1)
,
\gamma_{t}=\frac{1}{L_{yy}+\beta_{t}}
and
\max\{\beta_{t},\mu\}>0
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumption

1.1
holds, then for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle H_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq(L_{yy}-\mu)\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}-\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}\right)\|y_{t+1}-y_{t+1}^{\star}(x_{t+1})\|^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}.

Proof.

From
Lemma

A.5
,
Lemma

A.13
as well as
\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|\leq D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle{\cal L}_{t}(x_{t},y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t}))-{\cal L}_{t}(x_{t},y_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{yy}-\mu}{2}\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{L_{yy}+\beta_{t}}{2}\|y_{t+1}-y_{t+1}^{\star}(x_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})\left(\frac{L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}\right)\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t+1}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}},

and

\displaystyle{\cal L}_{t}(x_{t},y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t}))-{\cal L}_{t}(x_{t},y_{t+1})

\displaystyle\geq H_{t+1}-\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t+1}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}-2L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}.

Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle H_{t+1}-\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)\left(1+\frac{L_{yy}+\beta_{t}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}\right)\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t+1}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{yy}-\mu}{2}\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{L_{yy}+\beta_{t}}{2}\|y_{t+1}-y_{t+1}^{\star}(x_{t+1})\|^{2}+2L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2},

which is equivalent to

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{H_{t+1}}-\frac{(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3/2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{yy}-\mu}{2}\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{L_{yy}+\beta_{t}}{2}\|y_{t+1}-y_{t+1}^{\star}(x_{t+1})\|^{2}+2L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{2(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}},

Using the fact that
2(a^{2}+b^{2})\geq(a+b)^{2}
for

\displaystyle a

\displaystyle=\sqrt{H_{t+1}}-\frac{(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3/2}},

\displaystyle b

\displaystyle=\frac{(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3/2}},

we conclude the proof.
∎

Lemma

D.2
uses
Lemma

D.1
to provide a bound on the accumulated dual gap terms, as well as a bound on the accumulated error terms
\|y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})-y_{t}\|
, which will later be used to bound the accumulated primal gap terms.

Lemma D.2
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

2
under
\tau_{t}\in(0,1)
,
\gamma_{t}=\frac{1}{L_{yy}+\beta_{t}}
and
\max\{\beta_{t},\mu\}>0
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumption

1.1
holds, then for any
T\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in[T]}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq(L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t+1}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}},

and

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}\|y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})-y_{t}\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{\beta_{t-1}+\mu}\right)\left((L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\tau_{t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{\beta_{t-1}+\mu}\right)\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}.

Proof.

By summing up the inequality in
Lemma

D.1
with
t=0,\cdots,T-1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in[T]}H_{t}

\displaystyle\leq(L_{yy}-\mu)\|y_{0}-y_{0}^{\star}(x_{0})\|^{2}-\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}(\mu+\beta_{t-1})\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}-(L_{yy}+\beta_{T-1})\|y_{T}-y_{T}^{\star}(x_{T})\|^{2}

(
32
)

\displaystyle+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\tau_{t}^{2}+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}.

Using
Lemma

A.6
and the fact that
\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}\geq 0
, we finish the proof for the first part.

Now we turn to the second part. By re-arranging (
32
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in[T]}(\beta_{t-1}+\mu)\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(L_{yy}-\mu)\|y_{0}-y_{0}^{\star}(x_{0})\|^{2}-(L_{yy}-\mu)\|y_{T}-y_{T}^{\star}(x_{T})\|^{2}-\sum_{t\in[T]}H_{t}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\tau_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}.

By the fact that
H_{t}\geq 0,\|y_{T}-y_{T}^{\star}(x_{T})\|^{2}\geq 0
and
\mu\leq L_{yy}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in[T]}(\beta_{t-1}+\mu)\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(L_{yy}-\mu)\|y_{0}-y_{0}^{\star}(x_{0})\|^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\tau_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}.

By Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}\|y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})-y_{t}\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}(\beta_{t-1}+\mu)\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{\beta_{t-1}+\mu}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{\beta_{t-1}+\mu}\right)\left((L_{yy}-\mu)\|y_{0}-y_{0}^{\star}(x_{0})\|^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\tau_{t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{\beta_{t-1}+\mu}\right)\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}},

which concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma

D.4
simplifies the sum terms appearing in the bounds from
Lemma

D.2
, using the parameter schedules in
Condition

D.3
.

Condition D.3
.

Sequences
\{\tau_{t},\beta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
satisfy

\tau_{t}=(t+1)^{-a}\quad\&\quad\beta_{t}=C(t+1)^{-b},

such that
\max\{C,\mu\}>0
for any
t\geq 0
.

Lemma D.4
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

2
under
\{\tau_{t},\beta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
satisfying
Condition

D.3
with
0<b<1
. If
Assumption

1.1
holds, then for any
T\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+2C)^{2}(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}})^{2}}{(C^{2}+\mu^{2})^{2}}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(\frac{C4^{b}}{(t+1)^{2a-3b}}+\frac{\mu}{(t+1)^{2a}}\right),

and hence, if
\frac{2a-1}{3}\leq b<\frac{2a}{3}
then

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t\in[T]}\|y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})-y_{t}\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(CT^{1+b}+\mu T\right)}{(C+\mu)^{2}}\left((L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{1}{(t+1)^{2a}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+2C)^{2}(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}})^{2}\left(CT^{1+b}+\mu T\right)}{(C^{2}+\mu^{2})^{2}(C+\mu)^{2}}\left(4^{b}C\begin{cases}\frac{T^{1-2a+3b}}{1-2a+3b}&2a-3b<1\\
\log(T)+1&2a-3b=1\end{cases}+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{\mu}{(t+1)^{2a}}\right).

Proof.

For any
t\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+C(t+1)^{-b}+C(t+2)^{-b})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(t+1)^{-a}+2CD_{{\cal Y}}((t+1)^{-b}-(t+2)^{-b})\right)^{2}}{(C(t+2)^{-b}+\mu)^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+2C)^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(t+1)^{-a}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}}(t+1)^{-(b+1)}\right)^{2}}{(C(t+2)^{-b}+\mu)^{3}},

where in the last inequality, we use
Lemma

F.2
. Moreover, using Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(C(t+2)^{-b}+\mu)^{3}}\leq\frac{1}{C^{3}(t+2)^{-3b}+\mu^{3}}\leq\frac{1}{(C^{2}+\mu^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(t+2)^{-3b}}+\mu\right)\leq\frac{1}{(C^{2}+\mu^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{C4^{b}}{(t+1)^{-3b}}+\mu\right).

Moreover, we also have

\displaystyle\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(t+1)^{-a}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}}(t+1)^{-(b+1)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(t+1)^{-a}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}}(t+1)^{-a}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}})^{2}(t+1)^{-2a}.

We conclude the proof by observing that

\displaystyle\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+\beta_{t}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\left(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}+2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(L_{yy}+\mu+2C)^{2}(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}})^{2}}{(C^{2}+\mu^{2})^{2}}\left(\frac{C4^{b}}{(t+1)^{2a-3b}}+\frac{\mu}{(t+1)^{2a}}\right).

This finishes the proof of the first part. Turning the second part, from Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we have that

\frac{1}{\beta_{t}+\mu}=\frac{1}{C(t+1)^{-b}+\mu}\leq\frac{1}{(C+\mu)^{2}}\left(\frac{C}{(t+1)^{-b}}+\mu\right),

which implies

\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{\beta_{t-1}+\mu}\leq\frac{1}{(C+\mu)^{2}}\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\frac{C}{t^{-b}}+\mu\right)\leq\frac{CT^{1+b}+\mu T}{(C+\mu)^{2}}.

Substituting this into
Lemma

D.2
and using the first part conclude the proof of the second part.
∎

D.1
Proof of
Theorem

3.1

Piecing all the results together, we visit the first convergence guarantee for
Algorithm

2
under the vanilla setting
Assumption

1.1
as shown in
Theorem

D.5
.

Theorem D.5

(Detailed version of
Theorem

3.1

(i)
,
(ii)
)
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

2
under
\{\tau_{t},\beta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
satisfying
Condition

D.3
with
0<b<a<1
. If
Assumption

1.1
holds and
1-2a+3b>0
, then for any
T\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t\in[T]}G_{\cal X}^{\LMO}(x_{t},y_{t})}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\max_{x\in{\cal X}}\left|f(x)\right|+CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{T^{1-a}}+\frac{L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(1-a)T^{a}}+\frac{L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(1-a+b)CT^{a-b}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}}{T^{(1-b)/2}}\sqrt{\frac{(L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(t+1)^{-2a}}{(1+b)C}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2^{b+1/2}L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(L_{yy}+2C)(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}})}{T^{a-2b}C^{2}\sqrt{1-2a+3b}},

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t\in[T]}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(t+1)^{-2a}\right)}{T}+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(1-b)T^{b}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2^{2b+1}(L_{yy}+2C)^{2}(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}})^{2}}{T^{2a-3b}(1-2a+3b)C^{3}}.

By optimizing over
a,b
, the optimal rate is
O\left(T^{-1/4}\right)
, which is obtained when
a=\frac{3}{4},b=\frac{1}{4}
. If
Assumption

1.1
additionally holds with
\mu>0
and
C=0
, then for any
T\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t\in[T]}G_{\cal X}^{\LMO}(x_{t},y_{t})}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\max_{x\in{\cal X}}\left|f(x)\right|}{T^{1-a}}+\left(L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{\mu}\right)\frac{1}{2(1-a)T^{a}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\left(\mu^{3}(L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+2(2\mu^{3}L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(L_{yy}+\mu))^{2})\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(t+1)^{-2a}\right)^{1/2}}{\mu^{2}T^{1/2}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t\in[T]}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{3}(L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+2(2\mu^{3}L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(L_{yy}+\mu))^{2})\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(t+1)^{-2a}}{\mu^{3}T}.

By optimizing over
a
, the optimal rate is
O\left(T^{-1/2}\right)
, which is obtained when
a=\frac{1}{2}
.

Proof.

The bounds on the dual gap follow from the first claim in
Lemma

D.2
and the first claim in
Lemma

D.4
. The bounds on primal gap follows from the first inequality in the second claim in
Lemma

A.6
, the second claim in
Lemma

D.2
and the second claim in
Lemma

D.4
.
∎

D.2
Proof of
Theorem

3.2

We investigate the convergence of
Algorithm

2
under convexity of the primal variable, i.e.,
Assumption

1.4
. We start with
Lemma

D.6
, which bounds the sub-optimality gap in terms of the
\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|
terms.

Lemma D.6
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

2
under
\{\tau_{t},\beta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
satisfying
Condition

D.3
with
0<b<a\leq 1
. If
Assumption

1.1
holds, then for any
T\geq 1
,

\displaystyle G_{\cal X}^{\Opt}(x_{T})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}}{T^{a}}\sum_{t\in[T]}\|y_{t-1}-y_{t-1}^{\star}(x_{t-1})\|+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(1-b)T^{a+b-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\left(L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{(C+\mu)^{2}}\right)}{2}\begin{cases}\frac{1}{(1-a)T^{2a-1}}&a\in(0,1)\\
\frac{\log(T)+1}{T}&a=1\end{cases}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(1-a+b)(C+\mu)^{2}T^{2a-b-1}}

Proof.

From
Lemma

A.7
, we have

\displaystyle f_{t+1}(x_{t+1})-f_{t+1}(x^{\star})+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t+1}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\tau_{t})\left(f_{t}(x_{t})-f_{t}(x^{\star})+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t}}{2}\right)+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}\beta_{t}}{2}+2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\tau_{t}\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|+\left(L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}\right)\frac{\tau_{t}^{2}}{2}.

Since
0<a\leq 1
, it holds that

\frac{(t+1)^{a}-1}{(t+1)^{a}}\leq\frac{t^{a}}{(t+1)^{a}},

for any
t\geq 0
. Combining this with
Equation

31
, we have

\displaystyle f_{t+1}(x_{t+1})-f_{t+1}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+2)^{b}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{t^{a}}{(t+1)^{a}}\left(f_{t}(x_{t})-f_{t}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{b}}\right)+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{a+b}}+\frac{2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|}{(t+1)^{a}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{2a}}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(C+\mu)^{2}(t+1)^{2a-b}}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(C+\mu)^{2}(t+1)^{2a}}

\displaystyle=\frac{t^{a}}{(t+1)^{a}}\left(f_{t}(x_{t})-f_{t}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{b}}\right)+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{a+b}}+\frac{2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|}{(t+1)^{a}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{(C+\mu)^{2}}}{2(t+1)^{2a}}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(C+\mu)^{2}(t+1)^{2a-b}}.

We also deduce that for any
t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle t^{a}\left(f_{t}(x_{t})-f_{t}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(t+1)^{b}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(t-1)^{a}\left(f_{t-1}(x_{t-1})-f_{t-1}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2t^{b}}\right)+2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\|y_{t-1}-y_{t-1}^{\star}(x_{t-1})\|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2t^{b}}+\frac{L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{(C+\mu)^{2}}}{2t^{a}}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(C+\mu)^{2}t^{a-b}}.

Using this for
t=1,\cdots T-1
, we have

\displaystyle T^{a}\left(f_{T}(x_{T})-f_{T}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(T+1)^{b}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\|y_{t-1}-y_{t-1}^{\star}(x_{t-1})\|+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2t^{b}}+\frac{L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{(C+\mu)^{2}}}{2t^{a}}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(C+\mu)^{2}t^{a-b}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sum_{t\in[T]}\|y_{t-1}-y_{t-1}^{\star}(x_{t-1})\|+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}T^{1-b}}{2(1-b)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\left(L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{(C+\mu)^{2}}\right)}{2}\begin{cases}\frac{T^{1-a}}{1-a}&a\in(0,1)\\
\log(T)+1&a=1\end{cases}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}T^{1-a+b}}{2(1-a+b)(C+\mu)^{2}}

where in the last inequality, we use
Lemma

F.3
. From
Lemma

A.1

(vi)
, we have

f(x_{T})-f(x^{\star})\leq f_{T}(x_{T})-f_{T}(x^{\star})+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(T+1)^{b}},

which concludes the proof.
∎

Given
Lemmas

D.2
and
D.6
, the convergence guarantee then follows immediately.

Theorem D.7

(Detailed version of
Theorem

3.2

(i)
)
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

2
under
\{\tau_{t},\beta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
satisfying
Condition

D.3
with
0<b<a\leq 1
. If
Assumptions

1.1
and
1.4
hold, then for any
T\geq 1
,

\displaystyle G_{\cal X}^{\Opt}(x_{T})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}}{T^{(2a-b-1)/2}}\sqrt{\frac{(L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(t+1)^{-2a}}{(1+b)C}}+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(1-b)T^{a+b-1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2^{b+3/2}L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(L_{yy}+2C)(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}})}{C^{2}}\begin{cases}\frac{1}{\sqrt{(1-2a+3b)}T^{2a-2b-1}}&2a-3b<1\\
\frac{\sqrt{\log(T)+1}}{T^{(1-b)/2}}&2a-3b=1\end{cases}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\left(L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{\mu L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{(C+\mu)^{2}}\right)}{2}\begin{cases}\frac{1}{(1-a)T^{2a-1}}&a\in(0,1)\\
\frac{\log(T)+1}{T}&a=1\end{cases}+\frac{CL_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{2(1-a+b)(C+\mu)^{2}T^{2a-b-1}}

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t\in[T]}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+4L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(t+1)^{-2a}\right)}{T}+\frac{CD_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(1-b)T^{b}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2^{2b+1}(L_{yy}+2C)^{2}(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}+2bCD_{{\cal Y}})^{2}}{T^{2a-3b}(1-2a+3b)C^{3}}.

By optimizing over
a,b
, the optimal rate is
\widetilde{O}\left(T^{-1/3}\right)
, which is obtained when
a=1,b=\frac{1}{3}
.

Proof.

The bound on the primal gap follows from
Lemma

D.6
and
Lemma

D.4
. The bound on the dual gap follows from
Theorem

D.5
.
∎

When
{\cal L}
is strongly concave in
y
, i.e.,
Assumption

1.1
holds with
\mu>0
, obtaining accelerated rates requires modified arguments. In particular, we provide a refined bound on the term
\|y_{t}-y_{t}^{\star}(x_{t})\|
in
Lemma

D.8
below. Since we assume
\beta_{t}=0
for the rest of this section, we use the notation
y^{\star}(\cdot)
for
y_{t}^{\star}(\cdot)
.

Lemma D.8
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

2
under
1\geq\tau_{t}\geq\tau_{t+1}\geq 0
,
\beta_{t}=0
and
\gamma_{t}=\frac{1}{L_{yy}}
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumption

1.1
with
\mu>0
holds, then for any
t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\|y_{t}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|

\displaystyle\leq\rho^{t}D_{{\cal Y}}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu(1-\rho)}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i\in[t]}\tau_{i-1}\right),

and

\displaystyle G_{\cal Y}(x_{t+1},y_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq(L_{yy}-\mu)\left(\rho^{t}D_{{\cal Y}}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu(1-\rho)}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i\in[t]}\tau_{i-1}\right)\right)^{2}+2\left(2L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{\mu}\right)\tau_{t}^{2},

where

\rho:=\sqrt{\frac{L_{yy}-\mu}{L_{yy}}}\in[0,1).

Proof.

From
Lemma

A.12
, we have that

\displaystyle 0\leq L(x_{t},y^{\star}(x_{t}))-L(x_{t},y_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{L_{yy}-\mu}{2}\|y_{t}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{L_{yy}}{2}\|y_{t+1}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2},

(
33
)

which implies

\|y_{t+1}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|\leq\rho\|y_{t}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|.

By triangle inequality and
Lemma

A.1

(iii)
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\|y_{t+1}-y^{\star}(x_{t+1})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|y_{t+1}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|+\|y^{\star}(x_{t})-y^{\star}(x_{t+1})\|

\displaystyle\leq\rho\|y_{t}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|+\frac{L_{yy}}{\mu}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\rho\|y_{t}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu}\tau_{t}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\|y_{t}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|}{\rho^{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|y_{t-1}-y^{\star}(x_{t-1})\|}{\rho^{t-1}}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu}\frac{\tau_{t-1}}{\rho^{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\|y_{0}-y^{\star}(x_{0})\|+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu}\sum_{i\in[t]}\frac{\tau_{i-1}}{\rho^{i}}

\displaystyle\leq D_{{\cal Y}}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu}\sum_{i\in[t]}\frac{\tau_{i-1}}{\rho^{i}}

We have from
Lemma

F.1
with
a_{i}=\frac{1}{\rho^{i}}
and
b_{i}=\tau_{i-1}
that

t\sum_{i\in[t]}\frac{\tau_{i-1}}{\rho^{i}}\leq\left(\sum_{i\in[t]}\frac{1}{\rho^{i}}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in[t]}\tau_{i-1}\right)=\frac{\rho^{-t}-1}{1-\rho}\left(\sum_{i\in[t]}\tau_{i-1}\right).

which concludes the proof of the first part. We have from
Lemma

A.5
that

\displaystyle L(x_{t},y^{\star}(x_{t}))-L(x_{t},y_{t+1})

\displaystyle\geq G_{\cal Y}(x_{t+1},y_{t+1})-\frac{L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}\sqrt{2}\tau_{t}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\sqrt{G_{\cal Y}(x_{t+1},y_{t+1})}-2L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq G_{\cal Y}(x_{t+1},y_{t+1})-\frac{LD\sqrt{2}\tau_{t}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\sqrt{G_{\cal Y}(x_{t+1},y_{t+1})}-2LD^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sqrt{G_{\cal Y}(x_{t+1},y_{t+1})}-\frac{LD\tau_{t}}{\sqrt{2\mu}}\right)^{2}-\left(\frac{LD\tau_{t}}{\sqrt{2\mu}}\right)^{2}-2LD^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}.

Therefore, from the fact that
2(a^{2}+b^{2})\geq(a+b)^{2}
, we have for any
t\geq 1

\displaystyle G_{\cal Y}(x_{t+1},y_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\sqrt{G_{\cal Y}(x_{t+1},y_{t+1})}-\frac{LD\tau_{t}}{\sqrt{2\mu}}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{LD\tau_{t}}{\sqrt{2\mu}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(L(x_{t},y^{\star}(x_{t}))-L(x_{t},y_{t+1})+\left(\frac{LD\tau_{t}}{\sqrt{2\mu}}\right)^{2}+2LD^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}\right)+2\left(\frac{LD\tau_{t}}{\sqrt{2\mu}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(L_{yy}-\mu)\|y_{t}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}-L_{yy}\|y_{t+1}-y^{\star}(x_{t})\|^{2}+2\left(2LD^{2}+\frac{L^{2}D^{2}}{\mu}\right)\tau_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(L_{yy}-\mu)\left(\rho^{t}D+\frac{LD}{\mu(1-\rho)}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i\in[t]}\tau_{i-1}\right)\right)^{2}+2\left(2LD^{2}+\frac{L^{2}D^{2}}{\mu}\right)\tau_{t}^{2},

where in the third inequality, we use (
33
).
∎

From
Lemma

D.8
, bounding the desired gap terms for the convergence guarantee is almost immediate.

Theorem D.9

(Detailed version of
Theorem

3.1

(ii)
)
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

2
under
\{\tau_{t},\beta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
satisfying
Condition

D.3
with
0<a\leq 1
and
C=0
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumption

1.1
with
\mu>0
and
Assumption

1.4
hold, then for any
T\geq 1
,

\displaystyle G_{\cal X}^{\Opt}(x_{T})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\rho L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}D_{{\cal Y}}}{(1-\rho)T^{a}}+\frac{2L_{yx}L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{\mu(1-\rho)}\begin{cases}\frac{1}{(1-a)^{2}T^{2a-1}}&a\in(0,1)\\
\frac{(\log(T)+1)^{2}}{T}&a=1\end{cases}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\left(L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+\frac{L_{yx}^{2}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}}{\mu}\right)}{2}\begin{cases}\frac{1}{(1-a)T^{2a-1}}&a\in(0,1)\\
\frac{\log(T)+1}{T}&a=1\end{cases}

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t\in[T]}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{3}(L_{yy}-\mu)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}+2(2\mu^{3}L_{xx}D_{{\cal X}}^{2}+(L_{yx}D_{{\cal X}}(L_{yy}+\mu))^{2})\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(t+1)^{-2a}}{\mu^{3}T}.

By optimizing over
a
, the optimal rate is
\widetilde{O}\left(T^{-1}\right)
, which is obtained when
a=1
.

Proof.

We have from
Lemma

D.8
that if
a\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in[T]}\|y_{t-1}-y_{t-1}^{\star}(x_{t-1})\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\rho^{t}D_{{\cal Y}}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu(1-\rho)}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i\in[t]}\frac{1}{i^{a}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\rho^{t}D_{{\cal Y}}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu(1-a)(1-\rho)t^{a}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}\rho}{1-\rho}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}T^{1-a}}{\mu(1-a)^{2}(1-\rho)}

If
a=1
then

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in[T]}\|y_{t-1}-y_{t-1}^{\star}(x_{t-1})\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\rho^{t}D_{{\cal Y}}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}}{\mu(1-\rho)}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i\in[t]}\frac{1}{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\rho^{t}D_{{\cal Y}}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}(\log(t)+1)}{\mu t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}\rho}{1-\rho}+\frac{L_{yy}D_{{\cal X}}(\log(T)+1)^{2}}{\mu(1-\rho)}

This concludes the proof of the bound on the primal gap by using
Lemma

D.6
. The bound on the dual gap follows from
Theorem

D.5
.
∎

Appendix E
Proofs of
Section

4

To start the analysis, recall that the primal quantity of interest here is
\frac{1}{\tau_{t}}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|
.
Lemma

A.10
provides
almost
a telescoping bound for the terms
\frac{1}{\tau_{t}^{2}}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}
except for a nuisance term

\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}},H_{t}\right\}.

Lemma

E.1
provides a further bound on this nuisance term, so that a telescoping sum can be applied to
Lemma

A.10
, which will be done in
Lemma

E.2
.

Lemma E.1
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

3
under
\tau_{t}>0
,
\beta_{t}\geq\beta_{t+1}\geq 0
and
\max\{\beta_{t},\mu\}>0
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumption

1.1
holds, then for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}},H_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq q_{t}-q_{t+1}+\left(2L_{xx}+\frac{4L_{yx}^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{1}{2\tau_{t}}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{4L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}

where for any
t\geq 0
,

q_{t}:=\max\left\{\frac{H_{t}}{2},H_{t}-\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{2(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}\right\}+\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}},H_{t}\right\}.

Proof.

Using
Lemma

A.2
and
Lemma

A.8
, we obtain

\displaystyle H_{t+1}-\frac{\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\sqrt{H_{t+1}}

(
34
)

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\frac{H_{t}}{2},H_{t}-\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{2(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}\right\}+2L_{xx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}.

If
H_{t+1}\leq(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}
then we have

q_{t+1}=H_{t+1}-\frac{H_{t+1}^{2}}{2(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}+\frac{H_{t+1}^{2}}{4(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}=H_{t+1}-\frac{H_{t+1}^{2}}{4(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}.

By AM-GM inequality for four numbers, we have

\displaystyle\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t+1}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}

(
35
)

\displaystyle=\left(\left(\frac{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/4}\sqrt{2D_{{\cal Y}}}\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\right)^{1/3}\right)^{3}\sqrt{\frac{H_{t+1}}{\sqrt{L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1}}D_{{\cal Y}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{H_{t+1}^{2}}{4(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}+\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/4}\sqrt{2D_{{\cal Y}}}\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\right)^{4/3}

\displaystyle=H_{t+1}-q_{t+1}+\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/4}\sqrt{2D_{{\cal Y}}}\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\right)^{4/3}.

Combining (
34
), (
35
) and defining

A_{t}:=\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/4}\sqrt{2D_{{\cal Y}}}\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\right)^{4/3}

give us

\displaystyle q_{t+1}\leq\max\left\{\frac{H_{t}}{2},H_{t}-\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{2(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}\right\}+2L_{xx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+A_{t},

which implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}},H_{t}\right\}\leq q_{t}-q_{t+1}+2L_{xx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+A_{t},

If
H_{t+1}>(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}
then we have

q_{t+1}=\frac{H_{t+1}}{2}+\frac{H_{t+1}}{4}=H_{t+1}-\frac{H_{t+1}}{4}.

By AM-GM inequality for two numbers, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{\beta_{t}+\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{H_{t+1}}{2}}

(
36
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{H_{t+1}}{4}+\frac{2\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}

\displaystyle=H_{t+1}-q_{t+1}+\frac{2\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}.

Combining (
34
), (
36
) and defining

B_{t}:=\frac{2\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}

give us

\displaystyle q_{t+1}\leq\max\left\{\frac{H_{t}}{2},H_{t}-\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{2(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}\right\}+2L_{xx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+B_{t},

which implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}},H_{t}\right\}\leq q_{t}-q_{t+1}+2L_{xx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+B_{t},

In both cases, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}},H_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq q_{t}-q_{t+1}+2L_{xx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}

(
37
)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/4}\sqrt{2D_{{\cal Y}}}\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\right)^{4/3}.

Using the inequality
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
and the fact that
\beta_{t}\geq\beta_{t+1}
, we have that

\frac{2\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}\leq\frac{4L_{yx}^{2}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t+1}-\beta_{t})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}

By AM-GM inequality for three numbers, we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/4}\sqrt{2D_{{\cal Y}}}\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\right)^{4/3}

\displaystyle=\left(\left(\frac{\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+L_{yx}^{-1}D(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})}{\sqrt{2\tau_{t}}}\right)^{2/3}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{2L_{yx}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/4}\sqrt{D_{{\cal Y}}\tau_{t}}}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\right)^{4/3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{3}\frac{\left(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+L_{yx}^{-1}D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{2\tau_{t}}+\frac{1}{3}\frac{16L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+2L_{yx}^{-2}D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{3\tau_{t}}+\frac{16L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{3(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}.

Substituting this bound into (
37
) concludes the proof.
∎

At this point, one may recognize that one major difference in analysis of
Algorithm

3
and the previous
Algorithms

1
and
2
is that we cannot follow the path where the bound on
H_{t}
is established first and the bounds on primal and dual gaps follow from there. Here, two sequences
\{H_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
and
\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|
intertwine as indicated by
Lemma

A.10
and
Lemma

E.1
and, hence, add additional complication for the analysis. However, such complication can be resolve as shown in
Lemma

E.2
.

Lemma E.2
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

3
under
\beta_{t}\geq\beta_{t+1}\geq 0
such that
\max\{\beta_{t},\mu\}>0
,
\tau_{t}\geq\tau_{t+1}>0
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumption

1.1
holds, then for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{4\tau_{t}}-\frac{5L_{xx}}{2}-\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}}{2(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

(
38
)

\displaystyle\leq r_{t}-r_{t+1}+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{8L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}},

where for any
t\geq 0
,
q_{t}
is defined as in
Lemma

E.1
and

r_{t}:=f_{t}(x_{t})+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t}}{2}+q_{t}.

Hence, if it additionally holds that

\displaystyle G_{1}

\displaystyle:=\inf_{t\geq 0}\left\{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{5L_{xx}\tau_{t}}{2}-\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}{2(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)}\right\}>0,

then

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left\|\frac{x_{t+1}-x_{t}}{\tau_{t}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sup_{t\geq 0}|r_{t}|}{G_{1}\tau_{T}}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{G_{1}L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{G_{1}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{8L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}}{G_{1}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}\right).

Proof.

Combining
Lemma

A.10
and
Lemma

E.1
gives us (
38
).

By dividing both sides of (
38
) by
\tau_{t}
summing up the inequality for
t=0,1,\dots,T
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{4}-\frac{5L_{xx}\tau_{t}}{2}-\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}{2(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)}\right)\left\|\frac{x_{t+1}-x_{t}}{\tau_{t}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r_{0}}{\tau_{0}}+\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{t}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{t-1}}\right)r_{t}-\frac{r_{T+1}}{\tau_{T}}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{8L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{0}}+\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{t}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{t-1}}\right)+\frac{1}{\tau_{T}}\right)\sup_{t\geq 0}|r_{t}|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{8L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sup_{t\geq 0}|r_{t}|}{\tau_{T}}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{8L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}\right).

By the definition of
G_{1}
, we finish the proof for the second claim.
∎

We note that if
\tau_{t}\leq\tau_{0}
for any
t\geq 0
then by using
(
Beck, 2017
, Theorem 10.9)
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{x_{t+1}-x_{t}}{\tau_{t}}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{\tau_{t}}\left(\PO_{\cal X}\left(x_{t}-\tau_{t}\nabla_{x}{\cal L}(x_{t},y_{t})\right)-x_{t}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\left\|\frac{1}{\tau_{0}}\left(\PO_{\cal X}\left(x_{t}-\tau_{0}\nabla_{x}{\cal L}(x_{t},y_{t})\right)-x_{t}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=:G_{\cal X}^{\PO}(x_{t},y_{t}),

Using the above results in this section, we arrive at somewhat generic bounds on the accumulated primal and dual gaps in
Lemma

E.3
but as soon as we introduce specific choices for
\{\tau_{t},\beta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
, the convergence rates follow immediately as shown in
Theorem

E.4
.

Lemma E.3
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

3
under
\beta_{t}\geq\beta_{t+1}\geq 0
such that
\max\{\beta_{t},\mu\}>0
,
\tau_{t}\geq\tau_{t+1}>0
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumption

1.1
and that
G_{1}
defined in
Lemma

E.2
is positive hold, then for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal X}^{\PO}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2\sup_{t\geq 0}|r_{t}|}{G_{1}(T+1)\tau_{T}}+\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{G_{1}L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{G_{1}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{8L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}}{G_{1}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}\right)\right)^{1/2},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{F_{1}}{T+1}\left(2\sup_{t\geq 0}|s_{t}|+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}\right)(U_{1}+1)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad+\frac{4(2U_{1}+1)L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}\right)\right)^{1/2}+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(T+1)}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\beta_{t},

where for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle U_{1}:=\sup_{t\geq 0}\frac{2L_{xx}+\frac{4L_{yy}^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{1}{2\tau_{t}}}{\frac{1}{4\tau_{t}}-\frac{5L_{xx}}{2}-\frac{12L_{yy}^{2}+L_{yx}^{2}}{2(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)}},\quad\&\quad F_{1}:=\sup_{t\geq 0}\max\{H_{t},(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\},\quad\&\quad s_{t}:=q_{t}+U_{1}r_{t}.

Proof.

The first claim follows immediately from
Lemma

E.2
and the concavity of the square root function. Now, we turn to the second claim. From the definition of
F_{1}
, we have

\min\left\{\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}},H_{t}\right\}\geq\min\left\{\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{F_{1}},H_{t}\right\}=\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{F_{1}}.

By using
Lemma

E.1
and
Lemma

E.2
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{4F_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq q_{t}-q_{t+1}+\left(2L_{xx}+\frac{4L_{yy}^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{1}{2\tau_{t}}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{4L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq s_{t}-s_{t+1}+\frac{(U_{1}+1)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4(U_{1}+1)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{4(2U_{1}+1)L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}

Thus, by summing the above inequality with
t=0,\cdots,T
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{H_{t}^{2}}{F_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq s_{0}-s_{T+1}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}\right)(U_{1}+1)D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{4(2U_{1}+1)L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}.

By using the convexity of
(\cdot)^{2}
for the left-hand side, we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\sum_{t=0}^{T}H_{t}}{T+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{F_{1}}{T+1}\left(s_{0}-s_{T+1}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}\right)(U_{1}+1)D_{\mathcal{Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{4(2U_{1}+1)L_{yx}^{4}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})D_{\mathcal{Y}}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}\right).

Using this and
Lemma

A.6
, we finish the proof for the second part.
∎

.

.

.

E.1
Strongly-convex dual domain

This section investigates accelerated rates for
Algorithm

3
under
Assumption

1.2
. The analysis is similar to the non-strongly convex result, except that some bounds can be refined further mainly by using
Lemma

A.9
. Specifically,
Lemmas

E.6
,
E.7
,
E.8
,
E.9
and
E.10
mirror
Lemmas

E.1
,
E.2
,
E.3
,
E.4
and
E.5
, respectively.

Lemma E.6
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

3
under
\beta_{t}\geq\beta_{t+1}\geq 0
such that
\max\{\beta_{t},\mu\}>0
,
\tau_{t}\in(0,1)
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumption

1.1
and
Assumption

1.2
hold, then for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{H_{t},\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{4\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq r_{r}-r_{t+1}+\left(\frac{1}{8\tau_{t}}+2L_{xx}+\frac{4L_{yx}^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{2^{18}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}

where

r_{t}:=\max\left\{\frac{H_{t}}{2},H_{t}-\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{8\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}\right)}\right\}+\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{H_{t},\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{4\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}\right)}\right\}

Proof.

Using
Lemmas

A.2
and
A.9
, we obtain

\displaystyle H_{t+1}-\frac{\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\sqrt{H_{t+1}}

(
39
)

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\frac{H_{t}}{2},H_{t}-\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{8\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}\right)}\right\}+2L_{xx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}.

By using the inequality
ab\leq(2a^{3/2}+b^{3})/3
, derived from applying the AM-GM inequality, we have

\displaystyle L_{yx}\left(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}L_{yx}^{-1}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t+1}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}{\sqrt{2}}\cdot\frac{4L_{yx}\left(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}L_{yx}^{-1}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\alpha(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{H_{t+1}}{4(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{2}}{3\sqrt{2}}\left(\frac{H_{t+1}^{3/2}}{8(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{3}}+\frac{16L_{yx}^{3/2}(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}L_{yx}^{-1}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1}))^{3/2}}{\alpha^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{3/2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)H_{t+1}^{3}}}{24\sqrt{2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})}+\frac{8\sqrt{2}L_{yx}^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/2}(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}L_{yx}^{-1}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1}))^{3/2}}{3\alpha^{1/2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)}.

By using the inequality
a^{3/2}b\leq(3a^{2}+b^{4})/4
, derived from applying the AM-GM inequality, we have

\displaystyle\frac{8\sqrt{2}L_{yx}^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/2}(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}L_{yx}^{-1}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1}))^{3/2}}{3\alpha^{1/2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)}

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{3}\frac{16\sqrt{2}L_{yx}^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{1/2}\tau_{t}^{3/4}}{\alpha^{1/2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)}\left(\left(\frac{\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}L_{yx}^{-1}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})}{4\sqrt{\tau_{t}}}\right)^{1/2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}L_{yx}^{-1}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{16\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{18}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}}{8\tau_{t}}+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}L_{yx}^{-2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{18}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle L_{yx}\left(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}L_{yx}^{-1}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t+1}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}

(
40
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)H_{t+1}^{3}}}{24\sqrt{2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})}+\frac{\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}}{8\tau_{t}}+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}L_{yx}^{-2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{18}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}

If

\frac{H_{t+1}}{2}\leq H_{t+1}-\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)H_{t+1}^{3}}}{8\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1}\right)}\iff\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)H_{t+1}^{3}}}{4\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1}\right)}\leq H_{t+1},

then

r_{t+1}=H_{t+1}-\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)H_{t+1}^{3}}}{16\sqrt{2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})}\leq H_{t+1}-\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)H_{t+1}^{3}}}{24\sqrt{2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})}.

Combining (
39
) and (
40
) gives us

\displaystyle r_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq r_{t}-\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{H_{t},\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{4\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}\right)}\right\}+\left(2L_{xx}+\frac{1}{8\tau_{t}}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{18}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}.

By AM-GM inequality for two numbers, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)}{\sqrt{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{H_{t+1}}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{H_{t+1}}{4}+\frac{2\left(L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|+D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})\right)^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}

(
41
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{H_{t+1}}{4}+\frac{4L_{yx}^{2}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu},

If

\frac{H_{t+1}}{2}>H_{t+1}-\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)H_{t+1}^{3}}}{8\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1}\right)}\iff\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)H_{t+1}^{3}}}{4\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1}\right)}>H_{t+1},

then

r_{t+1}=\frac{3H_{t+1}}{4}=H_{t+1}-\frac{H_{t+1}}{4}.

Combining (
39
) and (
41
) gives us

\displaystyle r_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq r_{t}-\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{H_{t},\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{4\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}\right)}\right\}+\left(2L_{xx}+\frac{4L_{yx}^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}.

Hence, we conclude the proof.
∎

Lemma E.7
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

3
under
\beta_{t}\geq\beta_{t+1}\geq 0
such that
\max\{\beta_{t},\mu\}>0
,
\tau_{t}\geq\tau_{t+1}>0
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumptions

1.1
and
1.2
hold, then for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\left(\frac{13}{16\tau_{t}}-\frac{5L_{xx}}{2}-\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}}{2(\beta_{t}+\mu)}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

(
42
)

\displaystyle\leq q_{t}-q_{t+1}+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}.

where for any
t\geq 0

q_{t}:=f_{t}(x_{t})+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t}}{2}+r_{t}.

Hence, if it additionally holds that

\displaystyle G_{2}

\displaystyle:=\inf_{t\geq 0}\left\{\frac{13}{16}-\frac{5L_{xx}\tau_{t}}{2}-\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}{2(\beta_{t}+\mu)}\right\}>0,

then

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left\|\frac{x_{t+1}-x_{t}}{\tau_{t}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sup_{t\geq 0}|q_{t}|}{G_{2}\tau_{T}}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8G_{2}L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{G_{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{3\alpha^{2}G_{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}\right).

Proof.

By AM-GM inequality for three numbers, we have

\displaystyle L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{2}}{\sqrt{2}}\frac{4L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|}{\alpha(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{H_{t}}{4(\beta_{t}+\mu)(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{2}}{3\sqrt{2}}\left(\frac{H_{t}^{3/2}}{8(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{3}}+\frac{16L_{yx}^{3/2}(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|)^{3/2}}{\alpha^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{3/2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{24\sqrt{2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})}+\frac{8\sqrt{2}L_{yx}^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{1/2}(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|)^{3/2}}{3\alpha^{1/2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{16\sqrt{2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})}+\frac{8\sqrt{2}L_{yx}^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{1/2}(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|)^{3/2}}{3\alpha^{1/2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)}.

By AM-GM inequality for four numbers, we also have

\displaystyle\frac{8\sqrt{2}L_{yx}^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{1/2}(\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|)^{3/2}}{3\alpha^{1/2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)}

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{3}\frac{16\sqrt{2}L_{yx}^{3/2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{1/2}\tau_{t}^{3/4}}{\alpha^{1/2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)}\left(\left(\frac{\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|}{4\sqrt{\tau_{t}}}\right)^{1/2}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}}{16\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{18}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{4}}.

By AM-GM inequality for two numbers, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|}{\sqrt{\beta_{t}+\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{H_{t}}{2}}\leq\frac{H_{t}}{4}+\frac{2L_{yx}^{2}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\min\left\{H_{t},\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{4\sqrt{2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})}\right\}+\left(\frac{1}{16\tau_{t}}+\frac{2L_{yx}^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2^{18}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq r_{t}-r_{t+1}+\left(\frac{3}{16\tau_{t}}+2L_{xx}+\frac{6L_{yx}^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}},

where the last inequality follows from
Lemma

E.6
. Recall from (
21
) that we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{t}}-\frac{L_{f_{t}}}{2}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq L_{yx}\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|\sqrt{\frac{2H_{t}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}}+f_{t}(x_{t})+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t}}{2}-\left(f_{t+1}(x_{t+1})+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\beta_{t+1}}{2}\right).

We deduce the first part

\displaystyle\left(\frac{13}{16\tau_{t}}-\frac{5L_{xx}}{2}-\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}}{2(\beta_{t}+\mu)}\right)\|x_{t+1}-x_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq q_{t}-q_{t+1}+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}.

By dividing both sides of (
42
) by
\tau_{t}
and summing the inequality for
t=0,\cdots,T
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{13}{16}-\frac{5L_{xx}\tau_{t}}{2}-\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}{2(\beta_{t}+\mu)}\right)\left\|\frac{x_{t+1}-x_{t}}{\tau_{t}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{q_{0}}{\tau_{0}}+\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{t}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{t-1}}\right)q_{t}-\frac{q_{T+1}}{\tau_{T}}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{0}}+\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\frac{1}{\tau_{t}}-\frac{1}{\tau_{t-1}}\right)+\frac{1}{\tau_{T}}\right)\sup_{t\geq 0}|q_{t}|+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sup_{t\geq 0}|q_{t}|}{\tau_{T}}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}},

which concludes the proof of the second part.
∎

Lemma E.8
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

3
under
\beta_{t}\geq\beta_{t+1}\geq 0
such that
\max\{\beta_{t},\mu\}>0
,
\tau_{t}\geq\tau_{t+1}>0
for any
t\geq 0
. If
Assumptions

1.1
and
1.2
hold, then for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal X}^{\PO}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2\sup_{t\geq 0}|q_{t}|}{G_{2}(T+1)\tau_{T}}+\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8G_{2}L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{G_{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)\tau_{t}}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{2}}{3\alpha^{2}G_{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}\right)\right)^{1/2},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}}{2(T+1)}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\beta_{t}+\left(\frac{2F_{2}\sup_{t\geq 0}|s_{t}|}{(T+1)(\beta_{T}+\mu)^{1/2}}\right)^{2/3}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{F_{2}}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left((1+U_{2})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\left(\frac{(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{1/2}}+\frac{4(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3/2}}\right)+\frac{2^{18}(1+2U_{2})L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{9/2}}\right)\right)^{2/3},

where for any
t\geq 0

\displaystyle U_{2}:=\sup_{t\geq 0}\frac{\frac{1}{8\tau_{t}}+2L_{xx}+\frac{4L_{yx}^{2}}{\beta_{t}+\mu}}{\frac{13}{16\tau_{t}}-\frac{5L_{xx}}{2}-\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}}{2(\beta_{t}+\mu)}},\quad\&\quad F_{2}:=\sup_{t\geq 0}\max\left\{\frac{4\sqrt{2}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t})}{\alpha},\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}}\right\},\quad\&\quad s_{t}:=r_{t}+U_{2}q_{t}.

Proof.

The first claim follows immediately from
Lemma

E.2
and the convexity of the function
\|\cdot\|^{2}
. Turning to the second claim, we have from the definition of
F_{2}
that

\min\left\{H_{t},\frac{\alpha\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{4\sqrt{2}\left(L_{yy}+\beta_{t}\right)}\right\}\geq\min\left\{H_{t},\frac{\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}}}{F_{2}}H_{t}\right\}=\frac{\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{F_{2}}

From
Lemma

E.6
and
Lemma

E.7
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{(\beta_{t}+\mu)H_{t}^{3}}}{F_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq r_{r}-r_{t+1}+U_{2}\left(q_{t}-q_{t+1}+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{2^{19}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}}+\frac{4D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}+\frac{2^{18}L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{4}},

which implies

\displaystyle\frac{H_{t}^{3/2}}{F_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{s_{t}-s_{t+1}}{(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{1/2}}+(1+U_{2})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\left(\frac{(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{1/2}}+\frac{4(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3/2}}\right)+\frac{2^{18}(1+2U_{2})L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{9/2}}.

By summing the inequality over
t=0,\dots,T
,we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\frac{H_{t}^{3/2}}{F_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{s_{0}}{(\beta_{0}+\mu)^{1/2}}+\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\frac{1}{(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{(\beta_{t-1}+\mu)^{1/2}}\right)s_{t}-\frac{s_{T+1}}{(\beta_{T}+\mu)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left((1+U_{2})D_{{\cal Y}}^{2}\left(\frac{(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{8L_{yx}^{2}\tau_{t}(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{1/2}}+\frac{4(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})^{2}}{(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{3/2}}\right)+\frac{2^{18}(1+2U_{2})L_{yx}^{6}(L_{yy}+\beta_{t+1})^{2}\tau_{t}^{3}}{3\alpha^{2}(\beta_{t+1}+\mu)^{9/2}}\right).

Using the convexity of
(\cdot)^{3/2}
,
Lemma

A.6
, and the fact that

\displaystyle\frac{s_{0}}{(\beta_{0}+\mu)^{1/2}}+\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\frac{1}{(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{(\beta_{t-1}+\mu)^{1/2}}\right)s_{t}-\frac{s_{T+1}}{(\beta_{T}+\mu)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{(\beta_{0}+\mu)^{1/2}}+\sum_{t\in[T]}\left(\frac{1}{(\beta_{t}+\mu)^{1/2}}-\frac{1}{(\beta_{t-1}+\mu)^{1/2}}\right)+\frac{1}{(\beta_{T}+\mu)^{1/2}}\right)\sup_{t\geq 0}|s_{t}|

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sup_{t\geq 0}|s_{t}|}{(\beta_{T}+\mu)^{1/2}},

we conclude the proof of the second part.
∎

Theorem E.9

(Detailed version of
Theorem

4.2

(iii)
,
(iv)
)
.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by
Algorithm

3
under

\tau_{t}=\frac{3}{4}\left(A(t+1)^{a}+\frac{5L_{xx}}{2}+\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}(t+1)^{b}}{2C}\right)^{-1},\quad\&\quad\beta_{t}=C(t+1)^{-b},

for any
t\geq 0
for some
A>0,C\geq 0
such that
\max\{C,\mu\}>0
, and
0<a,b<1
. If
Assumptions

1.1
and
1.2
hold, then for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal X}^{\PO}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{T^{(1-a)/2}}+\frac{\log^{1/2}(T+1)\one_{2+2b-2a=1}+1}{T^{\min\{2+2b-2a,1\}/2}}+\frac{1}{T^{1/2}}+\frac{\log^{1/2}(T+1)\one_{2a-4b=1}+1}{T^{\min\{2a-4b,1\}/2}}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{T^{b}}+\frac{1}{T^{(2-b)/3}}+\frac{1}{T^{2/3}}+\frac{\log^{2/3}(T+1)\one_{6a-9b=2}+1}{T^{\min\{6a-9b,2\}/3}}\right).

By optimizing over
a,b
, the optimal rate is
O(T^{-1/5})
, which is obtained at
a=\frac{3}{5},b=\frac{1}{5}
. If
Assumption

1.1
additionally holds with
\mu>0
and
C=0
then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal X}^{\PO}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{T^{(1-a)/2}}+\frac{\log^{1/2}(T+1)\one_{2a=1}+1}{T^{\min\{2a,1\}/2}}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal Y}(x_{t},y_{t})

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{T^{2/3}}+\frac{\log^{2/3}(T+1)\one_{6a=2}+1}{T^{\min\{6a,2\}/3}}\right).

By optimizing over
a
, the optimal rate is
O(T^{-1/3})
, which is obtained at
a=\frac{1}{3}
.

Proof.

This follows directly from
Lemma

E.8
.
∎

Theorem E.10

(Detailed version of
Theorem

.

Suppose
\{x_{t},y_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is the sequence generated by

under

\displaystyle\tau_{t}=\frac{3}{4}\left(A(t+1)^{a}+\frac{5L_{xx}}{2}+\frac{13L_{yx}^{2}(t+1)^{b}}{2C}\right)^{-1},\quad\&\quad\beta_{t}=C(t+1)^{-b},

for any
t\geq 0
for some

. If
Assumption

additionally holds with
\mu>0
and

then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}G_{\cal X}^{\Opt}(x_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{1}{T^{2/3}}+\frac{\log^{2/3}(T+1)\one_{6a=2}+1}{T^{\min\{6a,2\}/3}}+\frac{1}{T^{1-a}}\right)

By optimizing over

, the optimal rate is
\widetilde{O}(T^{-2/3})
, which is obtained at

.

and

.
