Appendix F
Auxiliary Lemmas

Lemma F.1

(Chebyshev’s rearrangement inequality)
.

Suppose there are two sequences of real numbers
a_{1}\leq\dots\leq a_{t}
and
b_{1}\geq\dots\geq b_{t}
. It holds that

t\sum_{i\in[n]}a_{i}b_{i}\leq\left(\sum_{i\in[t]}a_{i}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in[t]}b_{i}\right).

Proof.

Since one sequence is increasing while the other is decreasing, one have

\displaystyle(a_{i}-a_{j})(b_{i}-b_{j})\leq 0\iff a_{i}b_{i}+a_{j}b_{j}\leq a_{i}b_{j}+a_{j}b_{i}.

for any
i,j\in[t]
. By adding
t^{2}
inequalities, we obtain

2t\sum_{i\in[t]}a_{i}b_{i}=\sum_{i\in[t]}\sum_{j\in[t]}\left(a_{i}b_{i}+a_{j}b_{j}\right)\leq\sum_{i\in[t]}\sum_{j\in[t]}\left(a_{i}b_{j}+a_{j}b_{i}\right)=2\left(\sum_{i\in[t]}a_{i}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in[t]}b_{i}\right),

which concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma F.2
.

Given
\alpha,k>0
and
t\geq 0
, it holds that
(t+k)^{-\alpha}-(t+1+k)^{-\alpha}\leq\alpha t^{-(\alpha+1)}
.

Proof.

From mean value theorem, there exists
t<c_{t}<t+1
such that

\displaystyle(t+k)^{-\alpha}-(t+1+k)^{-\alpha}

\displaystyle=-\alpha(c_{t}+k)^{-(\alpha+1)}(t+k-(t+1+k))

\displaystyle=\alpha(c_{t}+k)^{-(\alpha+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha(t+k)^{-(\alpha+1)}.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma F.3
.

Given
s\geq 0
, it holds that for any
T\geq 1
, if
s\neq 1

\frac{(T+1)^{1-s}-1}{1-s}\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{t^{s}}\leq 1+\frac{T^{1-s}-1}{1-s}\leq\frac{T^{1-s}}{1-s},

and otherwise,

\log(T+1)\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{t}\leq\log(T)+1.

Proof.

For any
t\geq 2
, we have

\int_{t}^{t+1}\frac{dr}{r^{s}}\leq\frac{1}{t^{s}}\leq\int_{t-1}^{t}\frac{dr}{r^{s}}.

We note that the left-most inequality also holds for
t=1
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{T+1}\frac{dr}{r^{s}}\leq\sum_{t\in[T]}\frac{1}{t^{s}}\leq 1+\int_{1}^{T}\frac{dr}{r^{s}}.

By considering two cases:
s\neq 1
and
s=1
, we finish the proof.
∎

Lemma F.4
.

Given
\gamma\in(0,1]
,
2a>b,a,b\in(0,1]
, it holds that for any
t\geq\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\rfloor
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[t]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor+1}+\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-t}\frac{8E(a,b)}{\gamma t^{2a-b}},

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[t]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i^{2a}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor}+\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-t}\frac{8}{\gamma}\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{E(a,0)}{t^{2a}}&0<a<1\\
\frac{\log(t)}{t^{2}}&a=1\end{cases},

where

E(a,b):=\begin{cases}\frac{1}{1-2a+b}&2a-b<1\\
\frac{1}{2-2a+b}&2a-b\geq 1\end{cases}.

Proof.

We only prove the first part since the second one can be proved with the same strategy. If
2a-b\geq 1
, we break it down into

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\left[\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor-1\right]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}i^{-(2a-b)}+\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}i^{-(2a-b)}.

For the first component, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\left[\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor-1\right]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}i^{-(2a-b)}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in\left[\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor-1\right]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor+1}.

For the second component we observe that
a_{i}:=\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i}
with
\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t
form an increasing sequence since

\frac{a_{i+1}}{a_{i}}=\frac{1}{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\frac{i}{i+1}\geq 1\iff 1-\frac{1}{i+1}\geq 1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\iff i+1\geq\frac{2}{\gamma}.

Using
Lemma

F.1
with

\displaystyle a_{i}

\displaystyle:=\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i},\quad b_{i}:=\frac{1}{i^{2a-b-1}},

\displaystyle a_{i}

\displaystyle:=\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i^{1/2}},\quad b_{i}:=\frac{1}{i^{1/2}},

\displaystyle a_{i}

\displaystyle:=\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i},\quad b_{i}:=\frac{1}{i^{1/2}},

sequentially, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{t-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor+1}\left(\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i}\right)\left(\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\left(t-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor+1\right)^{2}}\left(\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i^{1/2}}\right)\left(\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\frac{1}{i^{1/2}}\right)\left(\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\left(t-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor+1\right)^{3}}\left(\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\right)\left(\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\frac{1}{i^{1/2}}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b-1}}\right).

Using the fact that

\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\leq\sum_{i\in[t]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-t},

and for any
2\leq k\leq t
,

\frac{1}{t-k+1}\sum_{i=k}^{t}\frac{1}{i^{s}}\leq\frac{1}{t-k+1}\int_{z=k-1}^{t}\frac{dz}{z^{s}}\leq\frac{t^{1-s}-(k-1)^{1-s}}{(1-s)(t-k+1)}\leq\frac{t^{1-s}-(k-1)t^{-s}}{(1-s)(t-k+1)}=\frac{1}{(1-s)t^{s}},

we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-t}\left(\frac{1}{t-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor+1}\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\frac{1}{i^{1/2}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{t-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor+1}\sum_{\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor\leq i\leq t}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-t}\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{t}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{(2-2a+b)t^{2a-b-1}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{8}{(2-2a+b)\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-t}\frac{1}{t^{2a-b}},

where the second inequality follows from
Lemma

F.3
. In summary, if
2a-b\geq 1
then

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[t]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-\left\lfloor\frac{2}{\gamma}\right\rfloor+1}+\frac{8}{(2-2a+b)\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-t}\frac{1}{t^{2a-b}},

If
2a-b<1
then by using
Lemma

F.1
with
a_{i}=\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}
and
b_{i}:=i^{-(2a-b)}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[t]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{i\in[t]}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-i}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{i\in[t]}\frac{1}{i^{2a-b}}\right)\leq\frac{2}{(1-2a+b)\gamma}\left(1-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)^{-t}\frac{1}{t^{2a-b}},

where the last inequality follows from
Lemma

F.3
. This concludes the proof of the first bound.
∎

Lemma F.5
.

Under
Assumption

1.1
, for any
t\geq 0
and
x\in{\cal X}
, we have

\|y_{t}^{\star}(x)-y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)\|\leq\frac{2D_{{\cal Y}}(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1})}{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}.

Proof.

From
Lemma

A.1

(i)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}{2}\|y_{t}^{\star}(x)-y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\cal L}_{t+1}(x,y_{t+1}^{\star}(x))-{\cal L}_{t+1}(x,y_{t}^{\star}(x))

\displaystyle\leq\nabla_{y}{\cal L}_{t+1}(x,y_{t}^{\star}(x))^{\top}(y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)-y_{t}^{\star}(x))

\displaystyle=\nabla_{y}{\cal L}_{t}(x,y_{t}^{\star}(x))^{\top}(y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)-y_{t}^{\star}(x))+\left(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1}\right)(y_{t}^{\star}(x)-y_{0})^{\top}(y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)-y_{t}^{\star}(x)),

where the second inequality follows from the concavity of
{\cal L}_{t}(x,\cdot)
. By definition of
y_{t}^{\star}(x)
and the concavity of
{\cal L}_{t}(x,\cdot)
, we have from the optimality condition that

\nabla_{y}{\cal L}_{t}(x,y_{t}^{\star}(x))^{\top}(y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)-y_{t}^{\star}(x))\leq 0.

From Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and the fact that
\|y_{t}^{\star}(x)-y_{0}\|\leq D_{{\cal Y}}
, we deduce that

\displaystyle(y_{t}^{\star}(x)-y_{0})^{\top}(y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)-y_{t}^{\star}(x))

\displaystyle\leq\|y_{t}^{\star}(x)-y_{0}\|\|y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)-y_{t}^{\star}(x)\|

\displaystyle\leq D_{{\cal Y}}\|y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)-y_{t}^{\star}(x)\|.

As a result,

\frac{\beta_{t+1}+\mu}{2}\|y_{t}^{\star}(x)-y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)\|^{2}\leq D_{{\cal Y}}\left(\beta_{t}-\beta_{t+1}\right)\|y_{t+1}^{\star}(x)-y_{t}^{\star}(x)\|,

which concludes the proof.
∎

Appendix G
Implementation Details

G.1
Dictionary Learning

Following the implementation details in
(
Boroun et al., 2023
, Appendix F)
, we generate the old dataset matrix
{\mathbf{A}}={\mathbf{D}}{\mathbf{C}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
where
{\mathbf{D}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times p}
is generated randomly with elements drawn from the standard Gaussian distribution whose columns are scaled to have a unit
\ell_{2}
-norm, and
{\mathbf{C}}=\frac{1}{\|{\mathbf{U}}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{V}}\|_{2}}{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}
where
{\mathbf{U}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times l}
and
{\mathbf{V}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times l}
are generated randomly with elements drawn from the standard Gaussian distribution. The matrix
\tilde{{\mathbf{C}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{q\times n}
is generated by adding
q-p
columns of zeros to
{\mathbf{C}}
. The new dataset
{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n^{\prime}}
is generated randomly with elements drawn from the standard Gaussian distribution. Additionally, we let
n=500,m=100,p=50,l=5,q=60,n^{\prime}=103,\delta=10^{-4},r=5,B=1
.

In terms of intialization, all the methods start from the same initial point
x_{0}=({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}_{0},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}_{0})
and
y_{0}=0
where
{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}
is generated randomly with elements drawn from the uniform distribution in
[0,0.1]
whose columns are scaled to have a unit
\ell_{2}
-norm, and
{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}_{0}=\mathbf{0}_{q\times n^{\prime}}
.

Turning to our algorithms, for
Algorithm

1
, we set
\tau_{t}=\left(\frac{2}{t+10}\right)^{4/5},\beta_{t}=\frac{10^{-2}}{(t+10)^{1/5}}
. For
Algorithm

2
, we set
\tau_{t}=\left(\frac{2}{t+10}\right)^{3/4},\beta_{t}=\frac{10^{-2}}{(t+10)^{1/4}}
and for
Algorithm

3
, we set
\tau_{t}=\frac{10^{2}}{(t+10)^{3/5}},\beta_{t}=\frac{10^{-2}}{(t+10)^{1/5}}
. Using the notations adopted by the corresponding papers, for R-PDCG
(
Boroun et al., 2023
, Algorithm 1)
, we set
\tau=\frac{1}{K^{5/6}}
and
\mu=\frac{10^{-2}}{K^{1/6}}
and for CG-RPGA
(
Boroun et al., 2023
, Algorithm 2)
, we set
\tau=\frac{1}{K^{3/4}}
and
\mu=\frac{10^{-2}}{K^{1/4}}
and
\sigma=\frac{2}{L_{yy}+\bar{\mu}}
where the maximum number of iterations
K
is set to at
10^{3}
. We note that the strong convexity modulus of
{\cal Y}=[0,B]
in this experiment is
1/B
and
L_{yy}=0
as derived in
Section

H.1
. For SPFW
(
Gidel et al., 2017
, Algorithm 2)
, we use the vanilla stepsize
\gamma=\frac{2}{t+2}
for any
t\geq 0
. For AGP
(
Xu et al., 2023
, Algorithm 1)
, we choose the parameters as suggested in
(
Xu et al., 2023
, Theorem 3.2)
. That is
b_{k}=0,\beta_{k}=\bar{\eta}+\bar{\beta}_{k},c_{k}=\frac{19}{20\bar{\rho}k^{1/4}}
and
\gamma_{k}=\frac{1}{\bar{\rho}}
where
\bar{\eta}=\bar{\rho}=0.05,\bar{\beta}_{k}=\bar{\rho}L_{y({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}^{2}+\frac{16\tau L_{y({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}^{2}}{\bar{\rho}c_{k}^{2}}-2\bar{\eta}

and
\tau=2\max\left\{\frac{19^{2}\left(L_{({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}+2\bar{\eta}-\bar{\rho}L_{y({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}^{2}\right)}{20^{2}\cdot 16\bar{\rho}L_{y({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}^{2}},2\right\}
where all the Lipschitz constants are derived in
Section

H.1
. We also run each algorithms
300
seconds.

G.2
Robust Multiclass Classification

In these experiments, we choose
r=10,\lambda=\frac{\lambda^{\prime}}{2n^{2}}
where
\lambda^{\prime}=10
. For the intialization, we let
\Theta_{0}=\mathbf{0}_{k\times d}
and
y_{0}=\frac{1}{n}\one_{n}
. Turning to our algorithms, for
Algorithm

1
and
Algorithm

2
, we set
\tau_{t}=\frac{2}{t+10},\beta_{t}=0
. For
Algorithm

3
, we set
\tau_{t}=\frac{10^{3}}{(t+1)^{1/2}},\beta_{t}=0
. Using the notations adopted by the corresponding papers, for R-PDCG
(
Boroun et al., 2023
, Algorithm 1)
, we set
\tau=\frac{10}{K^{3/4}}
and
\mu=0
and for CG-RPGA
(
Boroun et al., 2023
, Algorithm 2)
, we set
\tau=\frac{10}{K^{1/2}}
,
\mu=0
and
\sigma=\frac{2}{L_{yy}+\bar{\mu}}
where
\bar{\mu}=\lambda
the maximum number of iterations
K
is set to at
10^{4}
. We note that
L_{yy}=\sqrt{2}\lambda^{\prime}
as derived in
Section

H.2
. For SPFW
(
Gidel et al., 2017
, Algorithm 2)
, we use the vanilla stepsize
\gamma=\frac{2}{t+2}
for any
t\geq 0
. For AGP
(
Xu et al., 2023
, Algorithm 1)
, we choose the parameters as suggested in
(
Xu et al., 2023
, Theorem 3.1)
. That is
b_{k}=c_{k}=0,\beta_{k}=\eta=2\max\left\{L_{\Theta\Theta},L_{y\Theta}^{2}\rho+\frac{4L_{y\Theta}^{2}}{\rho\mu^{2}}\right\}
where
\rho=\frac{\mu}{4L_{yy}^{2}}
and
\mu=\lambda
with all the Lipschitz constants are derived in
Section

H.2
.

Appendix H
Lipschitz constants

We devote this section to derive the Lipschitz constants inherited from dictionary learning and robust multiclass classification problems.

H.1
Dictionary Learning

Recall in the dictionary learning problem, we have

\displaystyle{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}),y)

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{2n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}+y\left(\frac{1}{2n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{F}^{2}-\delta\right)

\displaystyle{\cal X}

\displaystyle:=\{({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times q}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{q\times n^{\prime}}\mid\|{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}\|_{*}\leq r,\|d_{j}^{\prime}\|_{2}\leq 1,\forall j\in[q]\}

\displaystyle{\cal Y}

\displaystyle:=[0,B]

We have that

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}),y)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}^{\prime\top}-\frac{1}{n}y({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top},

\displaystyle\nabla_{{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}),y)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime\top}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}),y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2n}\|{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{F}^{2}-\delta.

For any pair
({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1}
and
({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2}
in
{\cal X}\times{\cal Y}
, we have that

\displaystyle|\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1})-\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2})|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2n}|\|{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{F}^{2}-\|{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{F}^{2}\||

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2n}|\Tr(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}-2{\mathbf{A}})^{\top}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2n}\|({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}-2{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}\|({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{F},

where the last inequality follows from Cauchy-Schwartz inequality. Using the fact that
\|{\mathbf{X}}{\mathbf{Y}}\|_{F}\leq\|{\mathbf{X}}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{Y}}\|_{F}
for any pair
({\mathbf{X}},{\mathbf{Y}})
with compatible dimension, we have

\displaystyle|\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1})-\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2n}\|({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}-2{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2n}(\|({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{F}+2\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F})\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2n}(\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}+2\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F})\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\sqrt{q}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}+\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F})\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}}{n}\|({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime})-({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})\|_{F},

where in the last inequality, we use the fact that
\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{F},\|{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{q}
.

Turning to partial gradient with respect to
{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}
, we have that

\displaystyle-\nabla_{{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1})+\nabla_{{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime})-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime\top}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}+{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime})^{\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime\top}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})

Using triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1})-\nabla_{{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\|{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\|{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{F})\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}q\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}

Now, we investigate the partial gradient with respect to
{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle-\nabla_{{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1})+\nabla_{{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}+\frac{1}{n}y_{1}({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime\top}-\frac{1}{n}y_{2}({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}-\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime\top}+\frac{1}{n}y_{1}({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}-\frac{1}{n}y_{2}({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}.

We have that

\displaystyle({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}-({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime\top}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime\top}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}-({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime\top}-{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top})

\displaystyle={\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}-({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime\top}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}+{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}-{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top})

\displaystyle={\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}-({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime})^{\top}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}

\displaystyle=({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}+{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}-({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top},

and

\displaystyle y_{1}({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}-y_{2}({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=(y_{1}-y_{2}){\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}-(y_{1}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-y_{2}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=(y_{1}-y_{2}){\mathbf{A}}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}-(y_{1}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-y_{2}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}+y_{2}{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-y_{2}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=(y_{1}-y_{2})({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}-y_{2}({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}

In summary, we have

\displaystyle-\nabla_{{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1})+\nabla_{{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}({\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})({\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})^{\top}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}({\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}){\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}

\displaystyle\quad-({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime})\left(\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}+\frac{1}{n}y_{2}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\right)+\frac{1}{n}(y_{1}-y_{2})({\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}})\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}.

Thus, from the triangle inequality and the fact that
\|{\mathbf{X}}{\mathbf{Y}}\|_{F}\leq\|{\mathbf{X}}\|_{2}\|{\mathbf{Y}}\|_{F}
for any pair
({\mathbf{X}},{\mathbf{Y}})
with compatible dimension, we have

\displaystyle\|-\nabla_{{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1})+\nabla_{{\mathbf{D}}^{\prime}}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\left\|\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime\top}+\frac{1}{n}y_{2}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}^{\top}\right\|_{2}+\frac{1}{n}|y_{1}-y_{2}|\|{\mathbf{A}}-{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{F}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\|{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{2})\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}(\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{n}y_{2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2})+\frac{1}{n}|y_{1}-y_{2}|(\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}+\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}\|_{F}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2})\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\left(\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}r^{2}+\frac{1}{n}B\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{n}|y_{1}-y_{2}|(\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}+\sqrt{q}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2})\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}),y_{1})-\nabla_{({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}{\cal L}(({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}),y_{2})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\left(\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}q\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\right)^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\left(\frac{1}{n^{\prime}}(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}+\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}\left(\frac{r^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{B\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2}}{n}\right)+\frac{1}{n}|y_{1}-y_{2}|(\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}+\sqrt{q}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2})\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\left(\frac{(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})^{2}}{n^{\prime 2}}\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{q^{2}}{n^{\prime 2}}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+3\left(\frac{(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})^{2}}{n^{\prime 2}}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}\left(\frac{r^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{B\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}+|y_{1}-y_{2}|^{2}\frac{(\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}+\sqrt{q}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2})^{2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}}\right)\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\left(\left(\frac{2(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})^{2}}{n^{\prime 2}}+3\left(\frac{r^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{B\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\right)\|{\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{2q^{2}+3(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})^{2}}{n^{\prime 2}}\|{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime}-{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\frac{3(\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}+\sqrt{q}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2})^{2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}}|y_{1}-y_{2}|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left\{\sqrt{\frac{2(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})^{2}}{n^{\prime 2}}+3\left(\frac{r^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{B\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}},\frac{\sqrt{2q^{2}+3(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})^{2}}}{n^{\prime}}\right\}\|({\mathbf{D}}_{1}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{1}^{\prime})-({\mathbf{D}}_{2}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}_{2}^{\prime})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{3}(\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}+\sqrt{q}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2})\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}}{n}|y_{1}-y_{2}|,

where in the second inequality, we use the fact that
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
and
(a+b+c)^{2}\leq 3(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2})
and in the third inequality, we use the fact that
a^{2}+b^{2}\leq(a+b)^{2}
for any non-negative tuple
(a,b)
.
Therefore, we can choose

\displaystyle L_{({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle:=\max\left\{\sqrt{\frac{2(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})^{2}}{n^{\prime 2}}+3\left(\frac{r^{2}}{n^{\prime}}+\frac{B\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}},\frac{\sqrt{2q^{2}+3(\|{\mathbf{A}}^{\prime}\|_{F}+2r\sqrt{q})^{2}}}{n^{\prime}}\right\}

\displaystyle L_{y({\mathbf{D}}^{\prime},{\mathbf{C}}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{3}(\|{\mathbf{A}}\|_{F}+\sqrt{q}\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2})\|\tilde{\mathbf{C}}\|_{2}}{n}

\displaystyle L_{yy}

\displaystyle:=0.

H.2
Robust Multiclass Classification

Recall in the dictionary learning problem, we have

\displaystyle{\cal L}(\Theta,y)

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i})))-\frac{\lambda^{\prime}}{2n^{2}}\|ny-\one_{n}\|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle{\cal X}

\displaystyle:=\{\Theta\in{\mathbb{R}}^{k\times d}\mid\|\Theta\|_{*}\leq r\}

\displaystyle{\cal Y}

\displaystyle:=\{y\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}\mid\|ny-\one_{n}\|_{2}\leq\rho\}

where
\lambda^{\prime}=2n^{2}\lambda
,
(a_{i},b_{i})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\times[k]
and matrices
{\mathbf{A}}_{i}
have their
j
th columns as

\text{col}({\mathbf{A}}_{i})_{j}=\begin{cases}a_{i}&j\in[k]\setminus\{b_{i}\}\\
-(k-1)a_{i}&j=b_{i}\end{cases}

We have that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\Theta}{\cal L}(\Theta,y)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}

\displaystyle\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(\Theta,y)

\displaystyle:=(\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{1}))),\dots,\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{n}))))-\lambda^{\prime}\left(y-\frac{1}{n}\one_{n}\right)

For any pair
(\Theta,y)
and
(\Theta^{\prime},y^{\prime})
in
{\cal X}\times{\cal Y}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(\Theta,y)-\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(\Theta^{\prime},y^{\prime})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[n]}\left(\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{1})))-\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i})))-\lambda^{\prime}(y_{i}-y_{i}^{\prime})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n]}2\left(\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{1})))-\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i})))\right)^{2}+2\lambda^{\prime 2}(y_{i}-y_{i}^{\prime})^{2}.

By the intermediate value theorem, for any
i\in[n]
, there exists
\Theta_{i}\in[\Theta,\Theta^{\prime}]
such that

\displaystyle|\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i})))-\log(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i})))|=\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta_{i}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta_{i}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}|\Tr\left((\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta){\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right)|\leq\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}\|\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta\|_{F},

which implies

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(\Theta,y)-\nabla_{y}{\cal L}(\Theta^{\prime},y^{\prime})\|_{2}\leq\left(2\sum_{i\in[n]}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\|\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta\|_{F}+\sqrt{2}\lambda^{\prime}\|y-y^{\prime}\|_{2}.

By the triangle inequality, we also have that

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\Theta}{\cal L}(\Theta,y)-\nabla_{\Theta}{\cal L}(\Theta^{\prime},y^{\prime})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}-\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}-\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right\|_{F}.

We observe that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}-\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n]}|y_{i}-y_{i}^{\prime}|\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n]}|y_{i}-y_{i}^{\prime}|\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\|y-y^{\prime}\|_{2},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}-\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\left|\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}-\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}\right|\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}.

By the intermediate value theorem, for any
i\in[n]
, there exists
\Theta_{i}^{\prime}\in[\Theta,\Theta^{\prime}]
such that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}-\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta_{i}^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\left(1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta_{i}^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))\right)^{2}}\left|\Tr({\mathbf{A}}_{i}^{\top}(\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}\|\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta\|_{F}.

Thus, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}-\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{1+\exp(\Tr(\Theta^{\prime}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}))}{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i\in[n]}y_{i}^{\prime}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\|\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\max_{i\in[n]}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\|\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta\|_{F}.

In summary, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\Theta}{\cal L}(\Theta,y)-\nabla_{\Theta}{\cal L}(\Theta^{\prime},y^{\prime})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\max_{i\in[n]}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\|\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta\|_{F}+\left(\sum_{i\in[n]}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\|y-y^{\prime}\|_{2}.

Therefore, we can choose

\displaystyle L_{\Theta\Theta}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{4}\max_{i\in[n]}\|{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}=\frac{(k-1)^{2}+(k-1)}{4}\max_{i\in[n]}\|a_{i}\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{(k-1)k}{4}\max_{i\in[n]}\|a_{i}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle L_{y\Theta}

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{(k-1)k}{2}\sum_{i\in[n]}\|a_{i}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle L_{yy}

\displaystyle:=\sqrt{2}\lambda^{\prime}.
